# M11.47 — Remaining Interpreter Attribution Ledger

Result: exhaustive decoder-backed ledger for `247008` remaining interpreter executions
after seven independently verified safe-memory forms were promoted.

The promoted forms are `0x00026A`, `0x003A0C`, `0x00389E`, `0x0003F0`,
`0x06193C`, `0x061954` and `0x061266`. Their exact dynamic counts are
`16384 + 7613 + 7124 + 4565 + 2430 + 2041 + 1890 = 42047`.

The profile is an M11.47 `BASIC_BLOCK_NATIVE` 600-frame
baseline. Each row is one executed PC; counts are dynamic executions,
not ROM-byte coverage. Predecessor/successor fields are bounded ROM
fallthrough/branch evidence from the existing `re_slice_decoder`.

## Final Pareto

The required categories collapse the terminal classes without losing any
execution. Semantic includes `PROMOTABLE_EXISTING_PROOF` and
`NEW_SEMANTICS_REQUIRED`; runtime/prefetch is zero after M11.43--M11.46.

Hottest remaining PCs are `0x03A8A4` (26887), `0x060310` (11489),
`0x06135E` (8192), `0x00D990` (3567), `0x002C12` (2784), `0x0038E0`

No generic `other` bucket is used. Every row is assigned to one of
the M11.47 terminal classes, with unresolved register-based memory
retained as `UNKNOWN_WITH_EVIDENCE` rather than guessed. `PROMOTABLE_EXISTING_PROOF`
is a semantic sub-class in the exhaustive remainder ledger; the final Pareto
reports it together with `NEW_SEMANTICS_REQUIRED` under `semantic`.
